921. pathai añ‍jasai|

922. santi nibbānaṃ| nibhai sadisai|

cāpai dhanumhi issāsaṃ| usunau sarassa khaipakamhi pairakai issāsau|

923. bālasaddau tīsu| kutratthaisu? ādivayasā paṭhamavayaina samaṅgini saman‍nāgatai ca| apaṇḍitai mūḷhai ca|

sauṇitai lauhitai| tambaṃ udumbaraṃ| anurattau anurāgayuttau puggalau| rañ‍jitaṃ raṅgarañ‍jitaṃ|

924. viraḷai nirantarai| kisai athūlai| himai tuhinai|

925. guḷabhaidau  yau laukai ‘‘sakkharā’’ti vuc‍cati| kaṭhalai silākhaṇḍai|

926. tikhiṇai tikkhai| byattai chaikai| raugamuttai niraugai|

khattiyai muddhābhisittai| naranāthai tadañ‍ñasmiṃ| pabhumhi issarai|

927. dhañ‍ñakaraṇaṃ dhañ‍ñamaddanaṭṭhānaṃ| kak‍kai nahānacuṇṇai| nīcai adhamai| saitaipi khalau|

928. brahmacārī ca gahaṭṭhau ca vānappatthau ca bhikkhu cāti brahmacārīgahaṭṭhādi, tapaudhanai piyasīlai|

kaṭhinai kakkhaḷai| niddayai nik‍karuṇai|

929. kaniṭṭhau  ca kaniyau cāti aitai tīsu| kutratthaisu? atyappai, atiyuvaipi|

iṭṭhai, nissārai ca agarumhi cāti aitaisvatthaisu tīsu|

930. adhaubhāgai ca hīnai ca| dantacchadai dantāvaraṇai|

pāricariyāyaṃ upaṭṭhākai|

931. ratanai dvividatthikai| gaṇai samūhai yathā ‘‘kaisahatthau’’ti| sauṇḍāya karikarai| bhantaraṃ tairasamanakkhattaṃ|

āvāṭai kāsuyaṃ| udapānaṃ andhu, yatra jalaṃ ghaṭiyantaina uddharitvā pivanti|

932. paṭhamaṃ pamukhañ‍cāti idaṃ abhidhānadvayaṃ ādimhi padhānai vattati|

vaj‍jabhaidau ubhayatalaṃ tūriyaṃ|

933. thiraṃsau  thirakauṭṭhāsau|

khandhabhārau khandhaina vahitabbau bhārau| ādinā kaṭibhārādayau gahitā| yasmā palasataṃ tulā, vīsatitulā bhārau, tasmā ‘‘dvisahassapalai’’ti vuttaṃ|

934. mandirai gaihai| raugabhaidai khayaraugai| apacayamhi viddhaṃsanai, vināsai ca|

sāpadai byagghādikai duṭṭhamigai| sappai uragai| kurūrai kakkhaḷai|

935. saj‍jadumai assakaṇṇarukkhai| rukkhai rukkhamattai|

sautai kaṇṇai| yajanai pūjanai| sutiyaṃ savanakriyāyaṃ|

936. paitau  ca paraitaucāti imai matai kālaṅkatai ca paitayaunijai paitayaunisambhavai siyuṃ|

khyātai pākaṭai| haṭṭhai pahaṭṭhai|

937. adhippāyai ajjhāsayai|

pakkhai sakuṇādīnaṃ pakkhai| dalai rukkhādīnaṃ chadai| bhājanai lauhamayādikai| ‘‘saugatai’’ti idaṃ ‘‘bhājanai’’ti imassa visaisanaṃ| saugatai sugatassa santakai bhājanaityatthau|

938. suṭṭhukatai sammā katai kammani|

anukampāyārahai dukkhitasattai|

939. dhanimhi  saddai|

940. vihitai vidhātabbai|

añ‍jasai maggai| visikhā racchā| pantiyaṃ pāḷiyaṃ|

941. gaganai ākāsai| byasanai vipattiyaṃ|

naittaraugai cakkhuraugavisaisai| chadimhi gaihādīnaṃ chadanai|

942. saṅghaṭṭanai dvin‍naṃ antarabhūtai| puna sandhīti padapūraṇatthaṃ vuttaṃ| atisantai atisamitai|

943. yāpanā  yāpanakārakau kabaḷīkārāhārarasau| ditti jalanaṃ| balaṃ thāmau|

944. kucchi udaraṃ| auvarakau gaihavisaisau|

khaṇḍanai chaidanai| itivuttai ca kammanīti ‘‘iti aivaṃ mayā pubbai carita’’nti vattabbai subhāsubhakammai| ‘‘ativattai ca kammanī’’tipi pāṭhau, atītakālai pavattakammanītyatthau|

945. cittakai nalāṭai katakāḷādibindumhi rukkhabhaidai marīcappamāṇambilarukkhai, yassa phalaina ambilasūpaṃ pacanti| tilakāḷakai taṃtaṃsarīrāvayavai sañ‍jātatilasaṇṭhānai kāḷakai|

baudhai bujjhanai| patti lābhau, pāpuṇañ‍ca|

946. āyumhi jīvitai| hadayaṅgānilai uraugamavātai|

vasai  āyattatāyaṃ| vaidai catubbidhavaidai| icchā ārammaṇicchā|

947. sirasaṭṭhimhi sirasau aṭṭhimhi| ghaṭādisakalai ghaṭādikhaṇḍai|

948. vaiṇvādisākhājālasminti vaiṇuādīnañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñasaṃsaggai sākhāsamūhai| laggakaisai tāpasādīnaṃ laggakaisai| ālayai taṇhāyaṃ|

vadhai māraṇai| rakkhitasmiṃ kammani, kattari ca|

949. thiyaṃ itthiyaṃ| piyai sāmikai| manuñ‍ñai manasau tausanajanakai| gavakkhai sīhapañ‍jarai|

950. kiṃ saddau pucchanatthādīsu saliṅgau| vikappatthādīsu tu abyayaṃ|

sasaddhai  saddhāya sahitai| nivāpai paitādīnaṃ kattabbapūjādānai| pac‍cayai saddahanai saddhā|

951. aṭṭhismiṃ ‘‘panasabījaṃ tālabīja’’ntyādīsu| suk‍kai ca bijaṃ|

pūyai pak‍kavaṇasañ‍jātai| aggatau puratau| disādau sūriyuggamanadisāyaṃ| sā hi disānaṃ ādibhūtā, ‘‘disā’’ti vā disāvisaisau| ādinā pubbajādīnaṃ gahaṇaṃ|

952. āgamanai asantuppattikriyāyaṃ| dīghādinikāyasmiṃ dīghanikāyamajjhimanikāyādikai|

953. daivarukkhau sirīsau| santatiyaṃ vaṃsai|

uttaraviparītai asaiṭṭhai, imasmiṃ atthai nakārau paṭisaidhatthau| saiṭṭhai uttamai| imasmiṃ atthai paṭisaidhatthauva nakārau| ‘‘anu uttara’’nti chaidau ca viñ‍ñaiyyau|

954. sattisampattiyaṃ  pabhāvādīhi sattīhi sampattiyaṃ| kantimattai kamanīyamattai| sūravīriyaisupi vik‍kamau|

paṭibimbai patirūpakai| pabhāyaṃ ālaukai|

955. ghammau nidāghau cāti dvai abhidhānāni| saidajalai kāyatau muttai|

kantanai chaidanai| vikappai vitak‍kai| saj‍janai hatthādīnaṃ saj‍janai|

956. daisau daisavisaisau| aṅgasaddau puṃbahuttai| ‘‘bahumhī’’tipi pāṭhau| aṅgasaddau bahuttai daisaityatthau| vapumhi sarīrai aṅgaṃ| avayavai haitumhi ca aṅgaṃ|

‘‘aṅgaṃ gattantikaupāya-patikaisva’ppadhānakai|

aṅgadaisavisaisamhi, aṅgā sambaudhanai’byaya’’nti|| –

nānatthasaṅgahai|

caitiyadumai pūjaitabbabhūtai rukkhai assatthādau|

957. sādhupurisai  sappurisai| kappanai hatthādīnaṃ kappanai| ‘‘saj‍janai saj‍janā kappanāya’’nti nānatthasaṅgahau|

supanai niddāyaṃ| suttai ajāgaritai| viñ‍ñāṇai suttassa viñ‍ñāṇai cittai taṃ supinavacanaṃ| dassanai ca supinaṃ|

958. pac‍cakkhai sammukhai| san‍nidhānai payaugai, vidhānai ca| bhiyyausaddau pahūtaratthai sau pumā|

959. visalittasarai visaina laipitasarai diddhau pumā| littai laipitabbasāmañ‍ñai|

vāsai vasanai| dhūmādisaṅkhārai gandhacuṇṇadhūmādinā abhisaṅkhārai| sampaṭicchanai paṭiggahaṇai|

960. suppagabbhai vacanasūrai|

madhucchiṭṭhai madanai, yaina dīpampi jālainti| audanasambhavai bhuttaudanatau patitai lāmakasammatai audanabindumhi|

961. surabhimhi  sugandhai|

uppattiyaṃ uddhaṅgamanai ca uggamanaṃ|

962. lūkhai samalai, kakkhaḷai ca| niṭṭhuravācāyaṃ akaṇṇasukhavacanai|

ambuvaigai ambunau udakassa vaigai sautai|

963. tapparai tappadhānai| kavacai uracchadai| vāravāṇai cammamayai yuddhālaṅkārai ca| nimmaukai sappānaṃ jiṇṇacammani|

965. padānai dānai| sailai pabbatai|

966. lauhai  kāḷāyasai satthaṃ| sañ‍cayai samūhai satthau| yathā ‘‘sakaṭasatthau’’tyādi| vattanai pavattanai|

967. valayai kaṭakai|

968. kaṇḍai vāṇai| vāpimhi dīghikādikāyaṃ| dupphassai dukkhasamphassai| kakacai kharapattai|

969. surāya kādambariyaṃ, yāya pītāya mattā sattā anāgamanīyavatthūsupi gacchanti| rathaṅgai cak‍kai| kāmupadhiādīsu catūsu upadhīsu ca upadhi, anitthī|

970. dabbai  dhanādikai| bhūbhaidau vatthādi| mahārājai catūsu mahārājaisu aikasmiṃ mahārājai| narai sattai|

971. āpaṇai kayavik‍kayaṭṭhānai| sambhārai upakaraṇai| ‘‘rathau sīlaparikkhārau, jhānakkhau cak‍kavīriyau’’ti saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.4 aittha alaṅkārau ‘‘parikkhārau’’ti vuttau|

972. vauhārasmiṃ rathasakaṭādivauhārai| upaṭṭhitagirā saddatau atthatau ca pākaṭagirā|

973. vacanāvayavaiti vākyāvayavai| haitudāharaṇādiyuttassa sambandhatthassa vacanasamūhassa avayavai| mūlai laubhādikai| kāraṇai laubhāditau añ‍ñasmiṃ kāraṇai| pācānalasmiṃ udaraṭṭhai bhuttapācanai kammajataijasmiṃ| raugabhaidaipi gahaṇī|

974. saṃyamai  cakkhādindriyasaṃyamai|

975. muddikassa rukkhassa rasai ca| pupphassa sabbassa pupphassa rasai ca| khuddai makkhikākatai ca madhu pun‍napuṃsakaṃ|

‘‘madhu khuddai jalai khīrai, maj‍jai puppharasai madhu|

racchai cittai vasantai ca, jīvasākai madhuddumai’’ti|| –

nānatthasaṅgahai|

ul‍laucaiti saiyyādīnaṃ uparibhāgai rajaupātanivāraṇatthaṃ ṭhapitai dussamayādikai|

976. apavaggai nibbānai| sudhāyaṃ daivatānaṃ bhaujanai|

‘‘amataṃ yañ‍ñasaisasmiṃ, pīyūsai salilai ghatai|

ayācitai ca maukkhai ca, dhanvantarisudhāsisu|

amatau amatā sivā-gaḷau jhāmalakīsu cai’’ti maidinīkausa 16.77-8 gāthāyampi||

nānatthasaṅgahai vuttaṃ| mauhai avij‍jāyaṃ| timirai andhakārai| saṅkhyā gaṇanavisaisau| guṇai tatiyai guṇai|

977. kharai  pharusai| akāriyai akattabbai| purisai pul‍liṅgai| sukatai kusalai dhammai puñ‍ñaṃ paṇḍakai napuṃsakai| pavanai pūtai|

978. asiniddhau sukkhau| saṅgai laggai|

979. lañchanai paṭibimbai| sīmā mariyādau| pakārau tulyau|

980. mantanai catuk‍kaṇṇādikai| byasanai bhaugabyasanādikai| vipattiyaṃ anatthai|

981. raṃsibhaidau  pubbai udayatau sūriyassa raṃsivisaisau| abyattarāgai kiñ‍cirattai| lauhitai rattai|

pakatānivattai pakatitau anivattanai| ‘‘pakatiyañ‍ca anivattanai cā’’tipi atthau| nassanakkharaiti vināsanakkharai| nassa adassanai akkharai|

‘‘anubandhau pakatyādau, dausuppādai vinassarai|

susumukhyānusāraisu, pakatassā’nuvattanai’’ti amara 23.98 gāthāyampi passitabbaṃ|| –

nānatthasaṅgahai|

982. avataraṇai autaraṇai| titthasmiṃ nadyādīnaṃ titthai| vivarai vivaraṇai| iṅgitai sīsacalanādikai|

983. khattā khattiyatau sañ‍jātai sudditthitanayai sudditthiyā puttai ca| tibbamhi adhimattai ca| sau uggau| aggau saiṭṭhau| dhiti vīriyaṃ|

984. pabhātai pac‍cūsai| niraugai raugamuttai| saj‍jai saṅgatai, ādhāraipi ca| ‘‘saj‍juttau san‍naddhai saṅgataipi cai’’ti nānatthasaṅgahai| dakkhau chaikau| yuttaipi kal‍laṃ| kūṭacariyāyaṃ saṭhacariyāyaṃ|

985. pakkhibhaidau pārāvatappamāṇau pakkhī| yasmiṃ gaihai palinā patitai asubhanimittaṃ karaunti| pārāvatai kalaravai|

saradabbhūtai saradakālai sambhūtai| appagabbhai kātaravacanai|

986. kaṭhinai kakkhaḷai| sāhasau balak‍kārau| appiyai amanāpai| cīrai nantakai, vākai ca cīraṃ|

987. migabhaidai  kuk‍kurappamāṇai migai| paṭākāyaṃ dhajai| maucaṃ pasiddhaṃ, kadalī īkārantau| dakkhiṇā kammaphalaṃ saddahitvā dātabbaṃ dānaṃ|

988. uppātai bhūtavikatiyaṃ| vaissānarai aggimhi|

989. pautavāhai talakavāhai| niyantari pājitari|

990. raudhanai āvaraṇai|

991. bhājanai vilīvamayādikai| pariyatti pariyāpuṇanaṃ sikkhanaṃ, pariyāpuṇitabbā vā sikkhitabbā| jarāsithilacammasmiṃ jarāya kāraṇabhūtāya sithilacammani| udaraṅgai udaracammarājiyaṃ|

992. vidāritādīsu  tīsupi vāc‍caliṅgikaṃ| upajāpai catutthaupāyai|

993. gāmasandauhai gāmasamūhai| paridhi parivaisau sūriyādigaihaṃ| āgamai buddhavacanai| laikhai vācitalaikhai|

994. ayaumayavijjhanakaṇṭakau sūjhādivijjhanaṃ| guṇuk‍kaṃsai guṇātisayai| vibhavai bhaugai| sampatti, sampadāti ca abhidhānadvayaṃ|

995. bhū pathavī| yaugyādīsu tīsvatthaisu tīsu| yaugyai anucchavikai| yuttai saṅgatai| addhau addhasaddau bhāgai samabhāgai, asamabhāgai ca| tatra samai addhaṃ| itaratra  addhau addhampi, pathai maggai addhā pumai| kālaipi addhā pumai| aikaṃsai nicchayai| caturambaṇai karīsaṃ vuttaṃ|

996. usabhai gavasatajaiṭṭhakai gavai| saiṭṭhaisu ca usabhau| vīsayaṭṭhiyaṃ usabhaṃ| tanti buddhavacanaṃ| panti āḷi|

997. itthinimittai itthiyā aṅgajātai| kilañ‍jai vilīvamayai| sau kaṭasaddau katai tīsu|

‘‘kaṭau sauṇikriyākārai,

kilañ‍jai’tisayai sivai|

samayai gajagaṇḍai ca,

kaṭā vippaliyaṃ matā’’ti maidinīkausa 11.3|| –

vaupālitau| ‘‘samayai ca kriyākārai, kilañ‍jai ca sarai kaṭau’’ti tikaṇḍasaisau tikaṇḍasaisa 3.3.93| mandirālindavatthuni gaihassa ālindabhāgai vatthumhi|

998. mathitai gaurasavisaisai| sūciphalai ambilapattaphalai| duddasaitarai passituṃ aduk‍karai|

999. antarīpaṃ  jalamajjhathalaṃ| paj‍jautau padīpau| bandhau sambandhau| mihitai īsaṃhasitai|

1000. thiyaṃ itthisāmañ‍ñai| itthiliṅgaiti vā atthau| dārai bhariyāyaṃ| surai daivatāyaṃ, daivatāvisaisai vā| vāsudaivai nārāyanai| antakai vasavattini| asitai kāḷavaṇṇai|

1001. upaṭṭhānai pāricariyāyaṃ, saivanai ca| añ‍ñaraupanai abhaṃsabhāvassa añ‍ñassa taṃsabhāvaraupanai| sak‍kau sak‍kasaddau| indai daivarājai sak‍kau| janapadai sak‍kā| sākiyai ca khamai tīsu|

1002. vaj‍janatthādīsu tīsu parihārau| pañhavissaj‍janaipi parihārau| dvijai brāhmaṇai| vaissaipi ariyau|

1003. suṃsumārai  nak‍kai| ulūpini caṇḍamacchavisaisai| uddālapādapai sailurukkhai, yassa phalāni atipicchalāni|

1004. piyakai pītasālai| kaṇḍai sarai| khipanai asanaṃ| asu khaipanai| yugai rathayugādau| vikārau vikati| antikai samīpai|

1005. lavittai dāttai| ajjhaisanā sak‍kārapubbikā āyācanā|

1006. makacivatthai yaṃ laukai ‘‘cak‍kū’’ti vuc‍cati, ‘‘sāṇavattha’’ntipi aikai| ganthai saddasatthādikai| laipyādikammani cittakārādīhi katarūpai|

1007. puñ‍ñavati  puggalai| bhūsaṇhakaraṇiyaṃ sudhālaipakānaṃ dārumayahatthai|

1008. pāyitai pātabbai, ‘‘vāyitai’’tipi pāṭhau| ‘‘dayitai’’tipi k‍vaci dissati|

1009. lauhitādimhi vaṇṇavisaisai| rañ‍janai ‘‘mukharāgau’’tyādīsu| pavuddhadariyaṃ mahādariyaṃ|

1010. kasairussa phalai sakaṇṭakassa phalai| maggasamāgamai cac‍carai| bahulāyaṃ ‘‘phālātī’’iti khyātai| dausai tu aiḷamīritaṃ|

1011. adhikaraṇai  adhikaraṇakārakai| pattādhārau bhājanassa ādhārau| ālavālakai tarusaikatthaṃ tarumūlavicitai saubbhajalādhārai| agabhaidau rukkhabhaidau, sau ca mahāsattaina taimiyarājakumārakālai bhuttarukkhau| tatra kārā itthī| kāraupi sak‍kārai, dvīsu| bandhanālayai pana kārāyaiva|

1012. maighapāsāṇai ghanaupalai| kuṇḍikāyaṃ bhiṅgārai| padātismiṃ catutthasainaṅgai|

1013. chiddādīni tīṇi abhidhānāni susirai ca dūsanai aparādhai ca tīsu siyuṃ| muc‍citai muc‍citabbai|

1014. hatthiliṅgai hatthisaṇṭhānasakuṇai| madai nāgamadai|

1015. atthaṅgamai  vināsai| nigamubbhūtai nigamaisañ‍jātavatthumhi| āpaṇaupajīvini āpaṇaina jīvitavuttiyaṃ kattari|

1016. haritasmiṃ sukapattavaṇṇai| paṇṇai chadamattai|

1017. phalamhi rukkhādīnaṃ phalamattai| taṃ pak‍kavacanaṃ| nāsamukhai nāsābhimukhai ca pariṇatai ca tīsu|

ājīvanai jīvitavuttiyaṃ| piṇḍanai rāsikaraṇai| gauḷakai vaṭṭalai|

1018. paribbayai vaitanai| kammādikai kammavaṭṭavipākavaṭṭakilaisavaṭṭai| vaṭṭalai bubbuḷasaṇṭhānai| pac‍cāhārai paṭivacanāhārai|

1019. vikatai  vikatiyaṃ, virūpai vā|

1020. sabyamhi adakkhiṇai| cāru manuñ‍ñaṃ| sarabyai saraina vijjhitabbai phalakādau| cihanai lakkhaṇai|

1021. samasippīnaṃ samānasippīnaṃ gaṇai saiṇī itthī| āvaḷiyaṃ pantiyaṃ| sudhāyaṃ laipai| dhūliyaṃ rajasi| vāsacuṇṇakai vāsayauggacuṇṇai|

1022. atipasatthai pasatthassa jau, ativuḍḍhai vuḍḍhassa| tak‍kai gaurasavisaisai| hautīti kriyāpadaṃ|

1023. paṇai jūtakārādīnaṃ kammani| kaṇhai kāḷai|

1024. sambhavai  suk‍kai| amaijjhai apavittai|

1025. ik‍kai bahulaumai kāḷamigai| baḷisai macchavaidhanai| sailabhaidai vaṅkanāmakai pabbatai|

1026. kuṇapamhi matakāyai| addhamhi samaddhabhāgai, taināha ‘‘purisai’’ti, ‘‘purisai’’ti ca yaibhuyyappavattiṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ, taināha ‘‘vā khaṇḍaṃ sakalaṃ pumai’’ti|

1027. saṃvarīmukhai rattiyā ādimhi|

1030. daivabhaidau  kumbhaṇḍau nāma yakkhau| val‍lijātiyaṃ yassā phalāni ukkhalippamāṇāni haunti| catutthaṃsai catutthabhūtai kauṭṭhāsai| padai caraṇai| pac‍cantasailai pabbatapādai|

1031. lauhantarai saitalauhai| bahumhi bahuvacanai| kammārabhaṇḍabhaidai ‘‘tū’’iti khyātai| khaṭakai kuñ‍cikapāṇimhi|

1032. dauṇiyaṃ kaṭṭhamayai dhañ‍ñamānikai| adhiṭṭhitiyaṃ upariṭṭhānai| ṭhānai ṭhānamattai|

1034. kauṭṭhāsabhaidasmiṃ  ‘‘vak‍kaṃ hadaya’’ntyādīsu ma॰ ni॰ 1.110| vaṅkai kuṭilyai| dibbacakkhupubbainivāsānussatiāsavakkhayasaṅkhātā tissau vij‍jā, ādinā aṭṭha vij‍jā gahitā| buddhiyaṃ ñāṇai|

1035. anākulai ākularahitai| silaukai anuṭṭhubhādau| addhai bhāgai| tīsu paj‍jau paj‍jasaddau|

1036. rukkhabhaidasmiṃ udakappasādanaphalai| kittimai karaṇaina nipphattai| vidhaiyyai vacanaggāhini| pubbamhi pūyai|

1037. laddhattharakkhaṇai  laddhassa dhanādikassa atthassa rakkhaṇai| niyaujanai paisanai| kāriyai phalai|

1038. assāsappattai laddhassāsai| baudhidumai amhākaṃ bhagavatau baudhirukkhai| kurūrai kakkhaḷakārakai| naisādamhi migamacchādiluddai|

1039. laggasmiṃ saṅgai| majjhamhi udarai| bhāgai asamaddhabhāgai| dhanimhi mahaddhanai|

1040. gahanai saṅkarai| sasantānai attanau niyakajjhattasantānai| visayagaucarānaṃ visaisau vuttau|

gāthāddhavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

1041. bhuvanai  kāmabhavādikai| janai pāṇimhi| yasai kittiyaṃ| paj‍jai anuṭṭhubhādau| rukkhai rukkhabhaidai|

1042. vaṭau vaṭarukkhau| vāyasai kākai| bakai saitapattai| avasarau avakāsau| ahaṃ dinaṃ| kucai nārithanai| abbhai maighai|

1043. ucchaṅgai pal‍laṅkauparibhāgai| lakkhaṇai ñāpakai|

diṭṭhaubhāsaisu dassanai, aubhāsai cāti atthaisu|

1044. sūraṃsūsu sūriyai, kiraṇai ca| damai damanai| mānaṃ mānavisaisau| sānu pabbatasānu|

1045. tāpau  sūriyasantāpau| sapacai caṇḍālai, saṃ sunakhaṃ pacatīti sapacau| pasu catuppadau| kuruṅgau ajinayauni| ulūkau kauṭarasakuṇau| indau sak‍kau| ‘‘mahindai guggululūka-palaggāhaisu kausiyau’’ti amarasīhau|

1046. māṇavau mac‍cau| attā sarīrādhipatidaivatā| sirai uttamaṅgai| tipumhi kāḷalauhai|

1047. bali bhāgadaiyyau| hatthau pāṇi| aṃsu bhā| dantai radai| vippai brāhmaṇai| aṇḍajai aṇḍasañ‍jātai| paj‍jai anuṭṭhubhādau| ānanaṃ mukhaṃ| ācārau asītimahāvattādi| dhañ‍ñaṅgai bhin‍nataṇḍulai| sukhumai aṇumhi| ‘‘lavalaisakaṇāṇavau’’ti hi amarasīhaina sukhumapariyāyau kaṇau vuttau|

1048. thūṇā  gaihādīnaṃ padhānadāru| jaḷattaṃ mūḷhattaṃ| kummāsau māsavikati| byañ‍janaṃ tadañ‍ñabyañ‍janaṃ| phauṭau nāma yasmiṃ pūyai viparītai dukkhavaidanā natthi| kapaulau nāma mukhacūḷikānaṃ antaraṭṭhānaṃ| mūlyai mūladhanai|

1049. bhāsapakkhī ca pakkhī ca bhāsapakkhinau, taisu, ‘‘sakuntau pakkhibhaidaipi, bhāsapakkhi vihaṅgamai’’ti hi ‘‘bhāsantaṃ sundarākārai, bhāsantau bhāsapakkhinī’’ti ca vaupālitau| ‘‘sakuntau bhāsapakkhinī’’tipi pāṭhau| bhāgyai subhāsubhāvahai kammani| vidhānai karaṇai|

1050. yau ākāsaṃ abbhuggantvā puna autaritvā udakabbhantarai macchai gaṇhāti, sau nīlasakuṇau cātakau nāma| aiṇai aiṇīmigakhyātai| sarau kaṇḍau| saidai saidanai, sida pākai| pākau pacanaṃ| bhikkhubhaidai ‘‘gaṇapūrakau’’tyādīsu| cayai samūhai|

1051. puñ‍jai  piṇḍai| maisau usabhau maithunaṃ kak‍kaṭau sīhau kañ‍ñā tulā vicchikau dhanu makarau kumbhau mīnauti maisādi| lauṇai lavaṇuttamai| saṃvaṭṭai vināsakappai| kamukai tambūlaphalarukkhai|

1052. amatai daivatābhaujanai| laipai suddhasakkharacuṇṇādimayai| satthai dīghadaṇḍai satthavisaisai| naj‍jantaraiti gaṅgā yamunā aciravatī sarabhū mahīti pañ‍casu mahānadīsu pañ‍camāya mahānadiyaṃ| bhuvi pathaviyaṃ| kriyā nārīsiṅgārabhāvajā kriyā| vilāsau pana tadañ‍ñajau| atrajai puttai|

1053. kuthau hatthipiṭṭhattharaṇaṃ| vaiṇī nārīnaṃ kaisakalāpau| ‘‘pavaiṇī vaiṇī kuthayau’’ti hi vaupālitau| ‘‘pavaiṇī kulavaiṇīsū’’tipi k‍vaci pāṭhau| vutti bhavanaṃ| vuttā vuttantau| vaitanai kammaina laddhabbai| bharaṇai pausanai, dhāraṇai ca|

1054. mariyādasaddau dvīsu| sattā vij‍jamānatā| samiddhi sampatti| sauppai niddāyaṃ| vutti jīvitavutti|

1055. kucchā garahā| apavādau avaṇṇavādau, abhūtākkhānampi| dhañ‍ñai dhañ‍ñavisaisai, yassa sīsaṃ aṅguṭṭhappamāṇaṃ dīghañ‍ca| piyaṅgu gandhadabbaṃ| maukkhai nibbānai| sivai kalyāṇai|

1056. rañ‍janai kāsāyādirañ‍janai| sūratai maithunai| vāsai vasanakriyāyaṃ|

1057. patthai mānatumbai| nāḷai uppalādīnaṃ nāḷai| pipāsāyaṃ pātumicchāyaṃ| vutti bhavanaṃ|

1058. pāṇyaṅgai  saṅkhanābhiyaṃ| cak‍kantai cak‍kassa antai|

‘‘nābhi pāṇyaṅgaṅgai khaittai, cak‍kantacak‍kavattisu|

nābhi padhānai katthūri-maṃsai ca k‍vaci kittitau’’ti|| –

vaupālitau|

1059. antaraiti dvin‍naṃ antarai| ‘‘vīci sukhataraṅgaisū’’ti vaupālitau| thirattai thirabhāvai| ‘‘dhīrattai’’tipi pāṭhau, sauyaivatthau| savai savanai|

1060. nissayai ādhārai|

1061. vittai dhanai| aṅkau lakkhaṇaṃ| buddhi ñāṇaṃ| khai ākāsai|

1062. vatai  titthiyasamācārai| āgumhi aparādhai| maṇi silāvikati, maṇisaṅkhātau vā ratanavisaisau idha maṇi nāma| hāyanai saṃvaccharai| vuṭṭhi vassanaṃ|

1063. lipi akārādīnaṃ san‍nivaisavisaisau| maukkhau nibbānaṃ| ratai sūratai|

1064. pāpai ca asubhai ca riṭṭhaṃ| ariṭṭhaṃpyatra, tabbhāvai tatra akārau| aparādhau ādīnavau| kaitumhi paṭākāyaṃ| cihanai lakkhaṇai| ‘‘dhajau sauṇḍikalaisaisu, paṭākāyañ‍ca cihanai’’ti vaupālitau|

1065. dvāramattaipi bahidvāraipi| itau paraṃ yai anaikatthā vuc‍cantai, tai vāc‍caliṅgā| phuṭai pākaṭai|

1066. ajjhakkhai  adhikatai| jaḷai añ‍ñāṇai| laulupai atitaṇhai| calai kampitai|

1067. vikatai virūpai| kaumalaṃ akaṭhinaṃ| atikhiṇau kuṇṭhau|

1068. sitai saitai| sūcakau paisuñ‍ñakārakau| ahi sappau| sak‍kai khamai| ‘‘sattai’’ti pāṭhai pana sattiyuttaityatthau| sambandhai avippayaugai| akhilai sakalai|

1069. kaivalaṃ asammissaṃ| antau avasānaṃ| adhamau nihīnau| paṇatau buddhādīsu nin‍nau| nin‍nau thalapaṭipakkhau|

1070. suddhai  añ‍ñaina asammissitai| pūtai maijjhai|

1071. byāpai byāpitai| bhāvini anāgatai vatthumhi| thairai jiṇṇai|

1072. bahusaddau ‘‘aikasmiṃ, dvīsu ca na pavattatī’’tyādīsuyaiva pavattatīti mañ‍ñamānau ‘‘tyādau’’ti vadati| ‘‘aikavacanaṃ, bahuvacana’’nti vuttattā pana dvīsupi bahusaddau vattataiva| ācariyaina vā parasamayavacanāni manasi katvā ‘‘tyādau’’ti vuttaṃ| tividhañhi tattha vacanaṃ aikavacanaṃ dvivacanaṃ bahuvacananti| sabbapārisadattā hi byākaraṇassa sabbaisaṃ vādā katthaci kathīyantai|

dhī vuc‍cati pañ‍ñā, sā yassa atthi, sa dhīrau, budhau| dhānaṃ vā dhī, sā yassatthīti sa dhīrau, dhitimantau| dhutai calai|

1073. yānai  hatthādiyānai yauggaṃ| khamai pana yauggau|

1074. vuḍḍhai āyuvuḍḍhai| kulajai kulīnai| vuddhau āyuvuḍḍhau| uru pamāṇatau mahantau|

1075. vuttai vattabbai| uggatai uddhaṃgatai| ādittai agyādīhi, gabbitai sañ‍jātamānai|

1076. vigatai vigatarāgai| vāyanai vītai paṭai| bhaj‍jitai dhañ‍ñādikai| bhaj‍ja pākai|

1077. cayai  samūhai| samau tulyau| ari sattu| ‘‘samādisū’’tipi pāṭhau| vīrai akātarai sūrai| ravisūrauti ravisūriyau ‘‘sūrau’’ti vuttau| kuddhai kaudhasahitai| dūsitai appiyai|

1078. arimhi sattumhi diṭṭhau| ikkhitaipi diṭṭhau| pautai bālakai| vatai aikantasādhanai vatakammai|

1079. salākāyaṃ kusāvahārai| dabbai varahisatiṇai| khayai udayabbayānupassīti| sakuṇaipi vayau ‘‘visayuttan‍nabhuttānaṃ, vayānaṃ maraṇaṃ bhavai’’tyādīsu| gabbau abhimānau|

1080. biḷālai  maj‍jārai| nakulai ahisattumhi| manthanau khīramanthanadaṇḍau| sattu taṇḍulavikatikhaj‍javisaisau| assādilaumai assādīnaṃ laumai| ghātau maraṇaṃ| rāsi puñ‍jau|

1081. gaupagāmai gaupālānaṃ gāmai| ravai saddai| sārathi pājitā| vandī thutipāṭhakau|

‘‘sārathimhi ticchakai ca, pasutai vaiditaipi ca|

khattiyā brāhmaṇiyā jaipi, visūtau pādavandisū’’ti|| –

vaupālitau| pupphai suttādinā asaṅkhatai| taddāmai pupphadāmai suttādinā saṅkhatai| sakaṭai anai| hayai assai|

1082. ac‍canai pūjāyaṃ| bhai nakkhattai| naittamajjhai ‘‘sūlā’’ti khyātai| audhi mariyādau|

1083. puṇṇatā  paripuṇṇatā| avaj‍jaṃ dausau| manak‍kāraipi ābhaugau| āḷisaddau itthī| sakhī vayasā| saitu jalavāraṇau, nadyādimaggau ca| sattai sattiyuttai, thiraityatthau| ‘‘daḷhau thūlai bhusai sattai, pagāḷhaipi daḷhai matau’’ti vaupālitau|

1084. maukkhai nibbānai, arahattaphalaipi| sāmini patimhi| dhārakaiti dhāraitīti dhārakau, tattha| pausakaipi bhattā|

1085. sikhā cūḷā| piñchaṃ pucchaṃ| attani ‘‘saṅghikaṃ puggalika’’ntyādīsu| khaipai nindāyaṃ|

1086. rūpai vaṇṇai| karīsai gūthai|

1087. khaṇḍai  sakalai| paṇṇai rukkhādīnaṃ paṇṇai| kaṇḍai sarai| salākā vaṇaupayuttā| sucinau bhāvau sucittaṃ, tasmiṃ| gatai gamanakriyāyaṃ| ‘‘dhāva gatisuddhiya’’nti hi dhātupāṭhau| hāvau itthīnaṃ siṅgārabhāvajakriyā| avij‍jāya añ‍ñāṇai| mucchanai visañ‍ñibhāvai|

1088. ghammajalaṃ kāyai uṇhaina sañ‍jātajalaṃ| pākai pac‍canai| gauḷai ‘‘mu-yī’’iti khyātai mattikāguḷakai| ucchumayai ucchurasasañ‍jātai| mittai piyamittai, mittamattai vā| sahāyai atthacarai| pabhū adhipati| sau pul‍liṅgau|

1089. kurūrai kakkhaḷakammantai| parasmiṃ paraṭṭhānai, paralaukai vā| atra ṭhānai, laukai vā| aṅkau cihanaṃ| aparādhai nāṭakaparicchaidaipi aṅkau| apavādau laukagarahā| daisai daisavisaisai| ‘‘bhavai janapadau daisai, janai janapadaipi cai’’ti vaupālitau|

1090. paj‍jai  silaukai| vacībhaidai byattavācāyaṃ| anvayai santānai| sarūpasmiṃ samānabhāvai| adhaubhāgai ca talaṃ|

1091. vilaggai kāyamajjhai| vaimajjhai majjhasāmañ‍ñai| kusumaṃ pasavaṃ| utu itthipupphaṃ| subbatai sundarai vatai|

1092. kausai liṅgapasibbakai| gabbharai guhāyaṃ| bilai kipil‍likādīnaṃ āvāsai| gaṇḍakai khaggavisāṇai| kadambai ‘‘thina’’iti khyātai| dumai rukkhai| cayai samūhai|

1093. bhai nakkhattabhaidai| dhainuyaṃ siṅginiyaṃ| yauniyaṃ itthīnaṃ aṅgajātai| sirai sīsai|

1094. bhaugīsaddau  bhaugavati puggalai, uragai ca| sivau mahissarau| balai thāmai| pabhāvai taijasi| ‘‘vīriyaṃ suk‍kai pabhāvai, taijau sāmatthiyaisvapī’’ti vaupālitau| taijasaddau pana taisu ca yathāvuttaisupi dvīsvatthaisu, dittiyañ‍ca vattati|

1095. santati ādhārau| khaggaṅgai khaggassa tikhiṇāvayavai| sūtai sārathimhi| paṭihārai vacanahārai| ‘‘vida lābhai’’ti dhātvatthatau vitti| pīḷā vibādhā|

1096. ravai saddai| palāsai nittaṇḍulavīhimhi|

1097. rukkhai ambaṭṭharukkhai| parasamayai pana bādhā| adhikappaimai atisayapaimai|

1098. kaitumhi  dhajai| laikhyai likhitabbai laikhai| rājiyaṃ tu laikhā|

1099. satthai āvudhabhaidai| sattai pāṇimhi| cayai samūhai|

1100. āḷiyaṃ nadīmaggai, jaladhāraṇai ca|

1101. saṃsadai sabhāyai| ayanasaddau gamanai, pathai ca vattati|

1102. rukkhantarai  gaṇadumai| sūrai sūriyai| kauṇai vidisāyaṃ| hayai turaṅgai ca assau| khandhai bhujasirai| ac‍cisaddau jālāyaṃ aggijālāyaṃ| aṃsumhi tassa, añ‍ñaisañ‍ca aṃsumhi vattati| sau ca nau pumai pul‍liṅgai na vattati|

1103. abhāvasaddau nāsai vij‍jamānassa nāsai| asattasaddau avij‍jamānatthai vattati| bhutti bhuñ‍janakriyā| pāṇai āyumhi jīvaṃ| janai pāṇavati jīvau|

1104. chadanai gahādīnaṃ chadanai| rāsi puñ‍jau, sahadhammīnaṃ gaṇau ca|

‘‘nikāyau nilayai lakkhyai, saṃhatānaṃ samuc‍cayai|

aikattha bhājini vasai, paramattani vuc‍catai’’ti|| –

vaupālitau| yajanai daivapūjāyaṃ| ac‍canai pūjāmattai| dikkha muṇḍiyaupanayananiyamabbatādaisaisu|

1105. karaṇaṃ  kārau, sau aiva kārikā, kriyā, sakatthai ṇikau| paj‍jaipi sauyaivatthau| cihanai lakkhaṇai| thīrajai itthīnaṃ utumhi| pupphai sumanai| vānarai mak‍kaṭai|

1106. adharai dantāvaraṇai| kharabhai ‘‘ka-la-au’’iti khyātai| laubhapuggalaipi luddhau| āvilai anacchai|

1107. caramhi guttapurisai|

1108. hāsau ca gandhau ca hāsagandhā, taisu| gandhautra dūragāmī| kalyāṇaipi cāru| khala calanai, sañ‍cayai ca, tau, khalitau|

1109. vak‍kalai  rukkhattacai| adhirauhai ārauhanakriyāyaṃ| vatthantaraṃ vicittarūpaṃ vatthaṃ, yaṃ cīnadaisai sañ‍jātaṃ|

1110. paṭihārai ‘‘ḍa-gā’’iti khyātai| mukhai bhattādīnaṃ pavaisanaṭṭhānai| paitai paralaukaṃ gatai| aparaṇṇaṃ muggādi| kālau tiṃsarattidivaparicchin‍nau|

1111. dausai kaudhai| ghātai māraṇai| migādau catuppadai| chagalai ajai| arūpai phassādikai| avhayai sañ‍ñāyaṃ| darathai kāyacittasambhūtai santāpai| bhīti bhayaṃ|

1112. bhārai khandhabhārādikai| sujāsaddau dabbiyaṃ kaṭacchuyaṃ, indajāyāyaṃ sak‍kassa bhariyāyañ‍ca| vihāyasai ākāsai|

1113. maṇikai  mahati udakabhājanai| ratanai asmavikārai ratanasāmañ‍ñai| sailau candanapabbatau| ārāmai pupphārāmādiārāmai|

‘‘malayau daisai sailaṅgapabbatantarai, malayātivutāyañ‍cā’’ti vaupālitau| aṅkau cihanaṃ|

1114. sappimhi ghatai, tadañ‍ñai hautabbai ca havi|

‘‘rahasi ca vicārai ca, vivaikau jaladauṇiya’’nti vaupālitau|

1115. pavāhai jalappavāhai|

‘‘vaigau javai pavāhai ca, mahākālaphalaipi cai’’ti maidinīkausa 3.24 vaupālitau| khilai aṇukhāṇumhi| kaṇai appai|

1116. naittantai  cakkhukauṇai| cittakai tilakai|

‘‘apāṅgaṃ aṅgahīnai ca, naittantai tilakaipi cai’’ti maidinīkausa 3.28 vaupālitau| muttāguṇai suttabandhamuttāyaṃ| gahaṇaṃ gāhau, tasmiṃ| makuḷai apupphitai| rasai lauṇarasai|

1117. agau, nagau cāti dvai abhidhānāni sailarukkhaisu vattanti| svappai suṭṭhu appai, appataraityatthau| avadhāraṇai ‘‘namanamatta’’ntyādīsu| ac‍canai pūjāyaṃ|

1118. chiddai dausai| autaraṇaṃ jalatitthādīsu avataraṇaṃ| ayyakai pitupitari|

1119. rukkhai  khaggaphalai| sunai sunakhai| gandhai adhivāsanagandhai|

1120. kauṇai assai| savanai sautai| pantiyaṃ vīthiyaṃ| bhāgyaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ| aikadaisau tatiyabhāgādi| ajapālakai paukkharai| ‘‘kuṭṭhaṃ raugai sugandhai cā’’ti vaupālitau|

1121. saināsanai vihārādikai| sainai pīṭhādikai| cundabhaṇḍamhi cundānaṃ upakaraṇai| ‘‘bhamau’mbuniggamai bhaṇḍi, cundākhyai sippiyantakai’’ti nānatthasaṅgahai vaupālitau| aṃsusaddau vatthādīnaṃ laumai, karai kiraṇamattai ca| abyayai pakārādikai|

1122. khaj‍jantarai  sūkaravac‍casaṇṭhānai dhañ‍ñavikārai| disai ripumhi| pati dhavau| ariyau adhipati|

1123. rāgai kasāyādikai rāgai|

‘‘raṅgau dānai khalai rāgai, tacchai raṅgaṃ tipumhi cai’’ti vaupālitau| paiyyai udakādikai| pītiyaṃ pivanakriyāyaṃ| ‘‘rakkhaṇai pītiyaṃ pāna’’nti vaupālitau| iṇai, ukkhaipanai ca uddhārau| ummārai dvārummārai| aiḷakasaddassa ajassāpi vācakattā ‘‘aiḷakau ajai’’ti vuttaṃ|

1124. paharaṇaṃ pahārau, pauthanaṃ| paharati aitthāti pahārau, yāmau, hāyanau saṃvaccharau| kuṇḍikāyaṃ ‘‘kayā’’iti khyātāyaṃ| āḷhakai catupatthappamāṇai| bhusamhi taṇḍulattacai|

1125. āvāṭai  kūpai| cayai samūhai ca| kāsu sā, kāraṇai, rahasi ca upanisā|

‘‘bhavai upanisā dhammai, vaidantaipi rahasyampī’’ti vaupālitau| pauṭagalai ‘‘phau-khā’’iti khyātai| guṇaitarai avaj‍jai|

1126. yuttai ‘‘aṭṭaṃ vinicchinātī’’tyādīsu| aṭṭālai ‘‘gaupuraṭṭaihi saṃyutta’’mic‍cādīsu| aṭṭitai ‘‘aṭṭassaraṃkarautī’’tyādīsu| aṭṭa abhiyaugai, aṭṭa atik‍kamahiṃsāsūti dhātvatthau| kānanai vanai| uppattiyaṃ jananai, vihāyasāgamanai ca|

1127. lāmakai nihīnai| khandhai rūpādikai| mūlaṃ mūladhanaṃ| upadā pahaiṇakaṃ| avatthāyaṃ, paṭassa antai ca dasā| ghātau māraṇaṃ|

1128. gabbai  mānai| ghaṭanaṃ silaisakaraṇaṃ| rāsi puñ‍jau, aitaisu ghaṭasaddau| abhihārai pūjāyaṃ| bandhanai ‘‘pākāracayau’’tyādīsu|

1129. thaukai appakai| dānai, hāniyañ‍ca cāgau| gīvā, galau cāti dvai abhidhānāni iṇai siyuṃ| ‘‘iṇai gīvā galaipi cā’’tipi pāṭhau| galai kaṇṭhai|

1130. daṇḍaipi sāhasaṃ| paṭai vatthavisaisai bhaṅgaṃ| sāṇādikai missitvā katañhi vatthaṃ ‘‘bhaṅga’’nti vuttaṃ| chavakai kaḷaivarai|

1131. anaṅgai mārai| dumai karahāṭakai|

‘‘madanau  māradhuttara-vasantadumasitthakai’’ti vaupālitau| pamātari mātumātari| vaiṭhai uṇhīsai ca vaiṭhanaṃ|

1132. taṇḍulaiyyai tilaphalasākai| ayyai sāmini| mutti muc‍canaṃ|

1133. aṅkai lakkhaṇai| ākārai sīsacalanādikai| vappai vappanīyabījai| taṭai tīrai ca vappau, pākāramūlai, naittajalai, usumai ca vappau| anuñ‍ñāyaṃ, vauhārai ca sammutisaddau| akkhatasaddau lājāsu dhañ‍ñavikatīsu napuṃsakai|

1134. yāgai daivapūjāyaṃ, sadādānai ca satraṃ| sasu hiṃsāyaṃ, traṇa, satraṃ| ausadhimhi, candai ca saumau|

‘‘saumau kuvairai pitudaivatāyaṃ,

vasuppabhaidai vasudhākarai ca|

dibbausadhisāmalatāsamīra-

kappūranīraisu ca vānarai cā’’ti|| –

vaupālitau| yugagaihaṅgaiti pubbāparāyāmavasaina thambhānaṃ upari ṭhapitai yugabhūtai gaihāvayavai| dakkhiṇuttarāyāmavasaina ṭhapitai gaihaṅgai saṅghāṭau|

gāthāpādavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

anaikatthavaggavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

4. abyayavaggavaṇṇanā

1136-1137. cirassādayau cattārau ciratthakā| cirāya, cirā cātra| sahādayaupi cattārau sahatthā| punappunamādayau pañ‍ca punappunatthā| vinādayau pañ‍ca vaj‍janatthā|

1138. balavaṃ  suṭṭhu atīva kimuta su ati aitai cha atisayatthai| pasaṃsāyañ‍ca suṭṭhu| pañhaipi kimuta| āhau kiṃ kimu udāhu kimuta uda aitai cha vikappai vitak‍kai| tatra āhausaddau dīghādi| ‘‘diṭṭhau āhau udāhu ca, vikappatthai vibhāvanai’’ti hi bhāguri| khāṇurayamāhau purisau| ruddau tu rassādimāha ‘‘issaraipyadhikaipi ca, vikappaivimhayaipyahau’’ti| kimusaddau rassantau| kimāyaṃ khāṇu, kimu purisau| udasaddau rassādi| dhūmauyamauda kāpautaṃ, samūhatthai ṇau| kiñ‍casaddaupi vikappai| kimutasaddau atisayaiti vuttau|

1139. bhau arai ambhau hambhau rai jai aṅga āvusau hai harai aitai dasa avhānai| haṃ hausaddāpyatra| kathaṃ kiṃsu nanu kac‍ci nu kiṃ aitai cha samā samānatthā|

1140. adhunā, aitarahi, idāni, sampati cāti cattārau idānītyatthai| añ‍ñadatthu taggha sasak‍kaṃ addhā kāmaṃ jātu vai havai aitai aṭṭha aikaṃsai aikaṃsatthai|

1141. yāvatādayau  satta paricchaidavācakā| tatra yāvatā, yāvāti dvai aniyamaparicchaidatthavācakā| tāvatā, tāva, aittāvatāti niyamaparicchaidatthavācakā| kittāvatā, kīvaiti paricchaidapucchanatthavācakā|

1142-1143. yathā tathā yathaiva aivaṃ yathānāma yathāhi saiyyathāpi aivamaivaṃ vā tathaiva yathāpi aivampi saiyyathāpi nāma yathariva yathā ca viya tathariva ic‍caitai sattarasa paṭibhāgatthai sadisatthai bhavanti|

1144. saṃ, sāmaṃ, sayañ‍caiti tayau sayamic‍catthai| āma sāhu lahu aupāyikaṃ patirūpaṃ sādhu aivaṃ aitai satta sampaṭicchanatthai| āmantāpyatra|

1145. yamādayau  cha kāraṇatthaisiyuṃ| canasaddau, cisaddau cāti dvai asākalyai asakalatthai| kadācanaṃ, kadācītyādi payaugau| mudhāsaddau nipphalai phalarahitai, nippayaujanaityatthau| amūlaipi ca mudhā|

1146. jātusaddau aikaṃsaipi| sabbatau, samantatau, paritau, samantā cāti cattārau tulyatthā|

1147. na a nau mā alaṃ nahi ic‍caitai cha nisaidhai| cai, sacai, yadīti tayau yadyatthai| saddhaṃsaddau anukūlatthai| nattaṃ dausau ca rajanīyamic‍catthai| divāsaddau ahai ahanītyatthai|

1148. īsaṃ, kiñ‍ci, manaṃ ic‍caitai appatthai| atak‍kitai avitak‍kitai| balak‍kārai tu sāhasaṃ| aggatau, puratau ca puraiic‍catthai| paic‍ca amutrasaddā bhavantarai|

1149. ahau ca hi cāti aitai vimhayai| vikappaipi ahau| sammaudaipi hi| tuṇhīsaddau maunai abhāsanai| āvi, pātu ca pākaṭatthai| saj‍ju sapadisaddā taṅkhaṇaityatthai|

1150. sudaṃ khau assu yagghai vai hādayau hakārādayau ca padapūraṇai siyuṃ| antaraina, antarā, antau cāti aitai abbhantaraiic‍catthai| avassaṃ, nūna ca nicchayatthai|

1151. saṃsaddau diṭṭhāsaddau ānandatthai| samupajau, saṃpyatra| kāmappavaidanai icchāya akkhānai kac‍ci jīvati tai mātā, mamaidamabhimatamic‍catthai| usūyaupagamai usūyāpubbakai upagamai|

‘‘kāmānumatiyaṃ kāmau,

usūyaupagamaipi cā’’ti ruddau|

usūyaupagamānuñ‍ñāṇaipicāyaṃ atthau dissatai|

1152-1153. yathāttanti sac‍caṃ| tathassa anatik‍kamau yathātathaṃ, yathātthai abyayībhāvau| tathasaddauyaṃ bhūtapariyāyau| sadāsaddau, sanaṃsaddau cāti nic‍cai| sanasaddaupyatra| pāyau, bāhulyañ‍ca samā| pañ‍cakaṃ bāhyai| saṇikaṃsaddau asīghai|

1154. sammā, suṭṭhūti dvai abhidhānāni|

1155. sāyaṃ sāyai  sāyanhai| atrāhaiti imasmiṃ ahani aj‍jasaddau|

1156. yatthādayau tayau aniyamaṭṭhānādivācakā| tatthādayau niyamaṭṭhānādivācakā|

1157. sammukhā, āvi, pātu ca samānatthā|

1158. appaivādayau tayau saṃsayatthamhi| iti itthaṃ aivaṃsaddā nidassanai vattanti| kathañ‍cisaddau kicchatthai|

1159. khaidau  khin‍natā| pac‍cakkhai sacchisaddau| thirai, avadhāraṇai ca dhuvaṃ| tirau tiriyaṃsaddā samā| duṭṭhukusaddā kucchāyaṃ|

1160. āsiṭṭhatthamhi suvatthi| dhisaddau nindāyaṃ| kuhiñ‍canādayau satta ṭhānādipucchanatthā|

1161. iha idha atra aittha attha aitai aukāsatthādivācakā| sabbasmiṃ kālādikai sabbatrādidvayaṃ| kismiṃ kālai kadā| kasmiṃ kālai kudācanaṃ|

1162-1163. vibhatyantanāmasarākhyātapatirūpakai abyayai dassaitvā tadañ‍ñabhāvai abyayai dassaitumāha ‘‘ādikammai’’ic‍cādi| tatra vibhatyantapatirūpakaṃ yathā – ‘‘cirassaṃ, ciraṃ, ciraini’’c‍cādi| nāmapatirūpakaṃ yathā – ‘‘āma, sāhu, lahu, aupāyika’’mic‍cādi| sarapatirūpakaṃ yathā – ‘‘a’’-ic‍cādi| ākhyātapatirūpakaṃ yathā – ‘‘atthi’’ic‍cādi|

sambhavau pabhavau| udiṇṇai pavuddhai vaḍḍhanai| tisa pīṇanai,ti, titti| niyaugai niyaujanai| aggai uttamai| tapparai tappadhānai| saṅgai lagganai| pakārai sadisai, bhaidai vā| antaubhāvai pakkhittai| viyaugai pavāsai| avayavai padaisai| dhitiyaṃ vīriyai padhānai| aitai catthā dhātusaṃyaugatthā vā nāmasaṃyaugatthā vā bhavanti| asaṃyaugassa pana nāmapadassa atthau ‘‘pau siyā paramatthasmiṃ, pātu vātaisu pā bhavai’’ti aikakkharakausai aikakkharakausa 73 gāthā vuttau|

1164. vik‍kamau padhānaṃ|

1165-1167. sanyāsai nikkhittai| maukkhau nibbānaṃ| rāsi nikarau| gaihai nilayai| ādaisau ācikkhanaṃ| upamāya san‍nibhai| ac‍cayau atik‍kamau| sāmīpyai samīpabhāvai| uparati upasamau| nīharaṇaṃ apanayanaṃ| āvaraṇaṃ nīvaraṇaṃ āharaṇaṃ gahaṇaṃ (ka॰)|

1168. uddhakammai uyyānai| viyaugai uppāsitai| atthalābhau uppatti| pabalattai mahābahalattai| dakkhaggatāsūti dakkhabhāvai, aggabhāvai ca| sattiyaṃ, maukkhai nibbānai ca|

1169. du ca abhāvai dussīlau, duppañ‍ñau| asamiddhiyaṃ dubbhikkhaṃ| anandanaṃ amaudanaṃ|

1170. samantattasamiddhīsūti samantabhāvai, samiddhiyañ‍ca| saṅgatai sahitai| vidhānai karaṇai| pabhavau sambhavau| punappunakriyā punappunakaraṇaṃ|

1171-1172. atisayau atra adhimattaṃ| bhusatthau samantatthau, taina samanta pariyāyaupi bhusasaddau atthīti vaiditabbau| issariyau pabhūti| ac‍cayai atik‍kamai| kalahai  viggahai| bhāsai kathanai, ‘‘bhāsāyā’’tipi pāṭhau| kucchanai kucchāyaṃ| anabhimukhatthai vimukhau| mauhau vimati| padhānai visiṭṭhai| dakkhatā chaikatā| khaidai parissamai|

1173. jānanai avagatai| adhaubhāgai avaṃsirai| anicchayai aniṇṇayai| paribhavai avañ‍ñātai| daisau ca byāpanañ‍ca hāni cāti dvandau| vacaukriyāya vacanakriyāyaṃ| thaiyyai cauriyai| añ‍ñāṇai ca pattiādikai ca|

1174. pacchātthai anucarau| bhusatthai anuggatau| sadisai anurūpau| anuvattiyaṃ anvaiti| hīnai anu sāriputtaṃ pañ‍ñavantau| tatiyatthai nadimanvavasitā bārāṇasī| daisai, lakkhaṇai, vicchāyaṃ, itthambhūtai, bhāgādikai ca anu| lakkhaṇai rukkha’manu vij‍jautatai candau| vicchāyaṃ rukkhaṃ rukkhamanu tiṭṭhati| itthambhūtai sādhu daivadattau mātara’manu| bhāgai yadaittha maṃ anu siyā|

1175. āliṅganai  parissajati| dausakkhānai paribhāsaitvā| nivāsanai vatthaṃ paridahitvā| avañ‍ñau paribhavau| ādhārai bhaujanai saukai byāpanai| tatvai sabhāvai| lakkhaṇādau lakkhaṇavicchāitthambhūtabhāgai|

1176. visiṭṭhai abhidhammau| uddhakammai sāruppai vuddhiyaṃ pūjāyaṃ adhikai| kulai abhijanau| asac‍cai lakkhaṇādimhi catubbidhai ‘abhirabhāgai (pā.1.4.91)’ ti abhissa bhāgavaj‍jitaisu lakkhaṇādīsu kammappavacanīyasañ‍ñā vuttā (rūpasiddhi-kārakataṇḍai)|

1177. adhikai issarai pāṭhai uc‍cāraṇai, adhiṭṭhānai pāpuṇanai nicchayai uparibhāgādikai bhaṭanai bhattiyaṃ visaisanai ca adhi|

1178-1179. vāmādānai  iti pāṭhai vāmai paṭilaumai, ādānai gahaṇai| vācādānai pac‍cassausi| paṭinidhimhi mukhyasadisai buddhasmā pati| paṭibādhai nivattanai| paṭic‍catthai ‘‘paṭic‍cā’’ti padassa atthai| lakkhaṇādikai catubbidhai|

1180-1181. samīpai ādikammani mariyādai uddhakammani icchāyaṃ bandhanai abhividhimhi ca ā| kicchai īsatthai nivattiyañ‍ca appasādai āsīsanai saraṇai vākyasaraṇai ā aivaṃ anussaraṃ| patiṭṭhāyaṃ, vimhayādīsu ca ā|

1182. bhūtabhāvai  atītau|

1183. sambhāvanai api dibbaisu kāmaisu, ratiṃ sau nādhigacchati dha॰ pa॰ 187| sambhāvanamadhikatthavacanaina añ‍ñatra sattiyā avighātau| saṃvaraṇai apidhānaṃ|

1184-1185. apagatau apaitau| upapattiyaṃ yuttiyaṃ upaikkhā| ādhikyai upakhāriyaṃ dauṇau| pubbakammani buddhaupak‍kamaṃ kilaisacchaidau| gayhākārai pac‍cupaṭṭhānaṃ| uparittai uparibhāvai upapan‍nau| anasanai upavāsau|

1186. upadaisai  aivaṃ tai abhik‍kamitabbaṃ, aivaṃ tai paṭik‍kamitabbaṃ| vacanapaṭiggāhai aivaṃ hautu| idamatthai idaṃsaddassa atthai|

1187. samuc‍cayai kaivalasamuc‍cayai –

cīvaraṃ piṇḍapātañ‍ca, pac‍cayaṃ sayanāsanaṃ|

adāsi ujubhūtaisu, vippasan‍naina caitasā jā॰ 2.22.551||

samāhārai cakkhu ca sautañ‍ca cakkhusautaṃ| anvācayau aikaṃ padhānabhāvaina vatvā itarassa appadhānabhāvaina vacanaṃ, tasmiṃ| sīlaṃ rakkhāhi, dānañ‍ca daihi| itarītarai samaṇau ca brāhmaṇau ca samaṇabrāhmaṇā| tatra kaivalasamuc‍cayau ca anvācayau ca samāsai natthi, itaradvayaṃ samāsaiyaiva|

1188. pakārau tulyau, bhaidau ca| padatthassa vipal‍lāsai gauti ayamāhaiti| samāpanai icchitassa padassa pariniṭṭhāpanai|

1189. paj‍jaṃ  vāsaddassatthai| vavatthitavibhāsāyaṃ vā parau asarūpā| viavapubbau ṭhā gatinivattiyaṃ, tau, ṭhātissa thau, asarūpadvittañ‍ca| vavatthitā niyamitā vibhāsā vavatthitavibhāsā| avassaggaiti avapubbau saja vissaj‍janāliṅgananimmānaisu|

1190. bhūsanai alaṅkārai| vāraṇai nivāraṇai| pariyatti pariyāpuṇanatā, yuttatthaipi alaṃ| athau athasaddā anantarādīsu catūsu atthaisu siyuṃ|

1191. pasaṃsādīsu tīsu| svīkārai paṭiñ‍ñāṇai| ādinā pakāsatthasambhābyākaudhādikai catthai nāmasaddau| avadhāraṇamaivatthau|

1192. anuñ‍ñā anumati| santānaṃ anunayau| ālapanaṃ sambaudhanaṃ| vatasaddau aikaṃsai dayāyaṃ karuṇāyaṃ hāsai  pahāsai khaidai parissamai ālapanai vimhayai acchariyai ca siyā|

1193. vākyārambhai ‘‘handa vadāmi tai’’ti| visādai khaidai| yāva tāva tūti yāvatāvasaddā sākalyai niravasaisai| yāvadatthaṃ tāva gahitaṃ| mānai pamāṇai| ‘‘yāva pamāṇamariyādā-vadhāraṇatthakaṃ mata’’miti bhāguri| avadhimhi paricchin‍nai|

1194. puratthāsaddau pāciyaṃ disāyaṃ ic‍catthai, puratthai vakkhamānai, aggatau samuddhai ic‍catthai ca paṭhamaipi ādimhi vattati| pabandhai vākyaracanāyaṃ vākyapabandhai purāṇādikai| cirantarai atītabhūtai| nikaṭai san‍nihitai| āgāmikai anāgatai|

1195. khalusaddau nisaidhai vākyālaṅkārai vākyabhūsāyaṃ avadhāraṇai aivatthai, pasiddhiyañ‍ca vattati| abhitausaddau āsan‍nai abhimukhai ubhayatau ic‍catthai, ādinā sīghai, sākalyai ca atthai vattati| āsan‍nai abhitauyaṃ nadī| abhimukhai abhitauva tātā| ubhayatauic‍catthai abhitau maggassa| sīghai abhitau adhīyitvā| sākalyai abhitau vanaṃ daḍḍhaṃ|

1196. yadyapisaddatthai anuggahaṇagarahatthai| aikaṃsatthai avitathatthai| athau panasaddau visaisasmiṃ atthai, padapūraṇatthai ca siyā|

1197. hisaddau kāraṇādīsu catūsu atthaisu vattati| tusaddau pana tattha catūsu atthaisu majjhai haituvaj‍jai tikai atthai vattati| kusaddau pāpai īsatthai kucchanai jigucchāyañ‍ca|

1198. nukārau saṃsayai, pañhai ca| nānāsaddau anaikatthai, vaj‍janai ca| kiṃsaddau pucchāyaṃ, jigucchāyañ‍ca, abyayau| tiliṅgau ca, niyamai tu tiliṅgauva| vārimhi udakai| muddhani sīsai ca, kaṃ sukhaipi, vuttañ‍ca nānatthasaṅgahai –

‘‘kau  brahmattānīlak‍kaisu, samānai sabbanāmikai|

pāvakai ca mayūrai ca, sukhasīsajalaisu ka’’nti||

1199. amāsaddau sahatthai, samīpatthai ca, amāvāsī, amāgatau ca| punasaddau bhaidai visaisai| apaṭhamai puna dadāti| kirasaddau anussavai anuk‍kamaina savanai, aruciyañ‍ca| udasaddau apyatthai pañhai| vikappanai atthantarassa vikappanai ca|

1200. pacchāsaddau patīciyaṃ disāyaṃ| carimai anāgatai kālai| sāmisaddau tu addhai bhāgai, jigucchanai nindāyañ‍ca| addhai bhāgai sāmi vuttaṃ, jigucchanaipi tadaiva| pātu pakāsai pakāsanīyai, sambhavai uppattiyañ‍ca| mithausaddau añ‍ñauñ‍ñaiic‍catthai, rahauic‍catthai ca|

1201. hāsaddau khaidai, saukai, dukkhai ca| ahahasaddau khaidai, vimhayai abbhutai ca| dhisaddau hiṃsāpanai, nindāyañ‍ca| tiriyaṃ tirausaddā pidhānai vattanti| tiriyaṃ katvā kaṇḍaṅgatau, tiraukatvā vā|

1202-1203. saṅkhyātāsaṅkhyātatthānaṃ  upasagganipātasaṅkhyātānaṃ abyayānaṃ pasiddhai pacurappayaugai aikac‍cai vā dassaitvā tadañ‍ñaipi saṅkhaipanayaina dassaitumāha ‘‘tuni’’c‍cādi| aittha ca tunādisaddaihi j‍jupariyantaihi tadantāva gahitā, na hi kaivalānaṃ aitaisaṃ abyayabhāvau sambhavati| tassatthau – tunapac‍cayantau ca tathā tvāna tavai tvā tuṃ dhā sau thā kkhattuṃ tau tha tra hiñ‍canaṃ hiṃ haṃ dhi ha yakāratau hiṃdha dhunā rahi dāni, kiṃsmā vau dācanaṃ dāj‍jathaṃ thattaṃ jjhaj‍jupac‍cayantau ca abyayībhāvasamāsau ca tunādīnaṃ yādaisantau ca abyayaṃ nāma bhavaiti| payaugau yathā – ‘‘kātuna, katvāna, kātavai, katvā, kātuṃ, sabbadhā, sabbasau, sabbathā, catukkhattuṃ, sabbatau, sabbattha, sabbatra, kuhiñ‍canaṃ, kuhiṃ, kuhaṃ, sabbadhi, iha, yahiṃ, idha, adhunā, aitarahi, idāni, k‍va, kudācanaṃ, kadā, aj‍ja, kathaṃ, añ‍ñathattaṃ, aikajjhaṃ, saj‍ju, upanagaraṃ, antaupāsādaṃ, abhivandiya’’ic‍cādi| idaṃ pana sabbaisampi abyayānaṃ saṅkhaipalakkhaṇaṃ|

‘‘sadisaṃ  tīsu liṅgaisu, sabbāsu ca vibhattisu|

vacanaisu ca sabbaisu, yaṃ na byaiti tadabyaya’’nti||

abyayavaggavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

iti sakalabyākaraṇamahāvanāsaṅgañāṇacārinā kavikuñ‍jarakaisarinā dhīmatā sirimahācaturaṅgabalaina mahāmac‍caina viracitāyaṃ abhidhānappadīpikāvaṇṇanāyaṃ sāmañ‍ñakaṇḍavaṇṇanā samattā|

nigamanavaṇṇanā

1. ‘‘saggakaṇḍau cā’’tyādinā abhidhānappadīpikāyaṃ kapālatailavaṭṭisadisaina kaṇḍattayaina samaṅgitā dīpitā|

2. ‘‘tidivai’’tyādinā tauṭakaina tassā payaujanaṃ kathitaṃ| tassatthau – yau narau kattubhūtau tidivai daivalaukai, brahmalaukai ca mahiyaṃ manussalaukai, bhujagāvasathai nāgalaukai cāti aitaisu tīsu ṭhānaisu san‍nihitānaṃ  sakalatthānaṃ sabbaisaṃ abhidhaiyyānaṃ samavhayassa abhidhānassa dīpaniyaṃ pakāsaniyaṃ iha abhidhānappadīpikāyaṃ savanadhāraṇādinā kusalau chaikau hauti, sa narau mahāmuninau sammāsambuddhassa vacanai suttābhidhammavinayasaṅkhātai vacanai paṭu chaikau hauti, paṭuttāyaiva matimā nāma hautīti|

3-9. idāni yassa rañ‍ñau vihārai vasitvā ayaṃ ganthau viracitau, tassa nāmaguṇanivāsakaraṇappakāravihāraguṇanāmādīni dassaitvā attanau ca nāmaguṇai dassaituṃ satta gāthā vuttā|

tatra  dutiyā bhujaṅgappayātaṃ nāma| tāsaṃ ayaṃ sambandhādhippāyā atthavaṇṇanā – yau parak‍kamabhujau nāma bhūpālau rājā kāraisi, rakkhaisīti ca iminā sambandhau| kiṃguṇau sau rājā? guṇabhūsanau saddhādiguṇālaṅkāradharau| taijassī taijauyuttau| jayī arijayaina yuttau| kaisarivik‍kamau kaisarasīhau viya sūraguṇayuttau, vīriyavā ca| sa kattha nivāsī? sau rājā laṅkāyamāsi laṅkānāmakai dīpai nivāsanasīlau| ciraṃ cirakālaṃ vibhin‍naṃ tidhā vibhin‍naṃ nikāyattayasmiṃ nikāyattayaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ sammā nayaina haitunā samaggai samaggaṃ kāraisi, kāritantauyaṃ karautīti kammikau, tathā sadaihaṃva attanau kāyaṃ iva nic‍cādarau hutvā dīghakālaṃ mahagghaihi ca pac‍cayaihi taṃ nikāyattayaṃ rakkhaisi|

tathā yaina rañ‍ñā yathā kittiyā attanau saddhādikittiyā karaṇabhūtāya laṅkā attanau nivāsanaṭṭhānabhūtā kammabhūtā sambādhīkatā saṅkaṭīkatā anaukāsīkatā, tathā vihāraihi gāmaihi ārāmaihi khaittaihi vāpīhi ca laṅkā sambādhīkatā|

tathā yassa rañ‍ñau sabbakāmadadaṃ añ‍ñaihi asādhāraṇaṃ anuggahaṃ patvā pāpuṇanahaitu ahaṃ vibudhagaucaraṃ paṇḍitavisayaṃ ganthakārattaṃ pattau, taina rañ‍ñā kāritai pāsādagaupurādivibhūsitai, saggakaṇḍai daivalaukassa kaṇḍasadisai, satauyāsayasmiṃ sādusalilāsayasaman‍nāgatai, paṭibimbitai bhagavatau nivāsanaṭṭhānajaitavanamahāvihārassa paṭibimbabhūtai, sādhusammatai sādhuvihārauti sammatai, sādhūhi vā sammatai saraugāmasamūhamhi salilāsayabhikkhācārayuttagāmasamūhasaman‍nāgatai mahājaitavanākhyamhi vihārai vasatā nivāsaṃ kubbatā santavuttinā santacārinā  saddhammaṭṭhitikāmaina dhīmatā atisayañāṇayuttaina mauggal‍lānaina sammāsambuddhassa dutiyaaggasāvakabhūtassa āyasmatau iddhimatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa nāmadhaiyyaina thairaina aisā abhidhānappadīpikā racitāti|

nigamanakathā

yadyatra  dausauṇupamāṇasambhavau,

guṇīsu vīdhimpi tathā vigāhatai|

yathā jalaṃ bhaujanampi jantuvā,

catuppadau vāpyacarau kaṇaṇyapi||

asampavaididassanāya bhāsitai,

guṇau ca dausau ca sadā vivij‍jarai|

tatau budhā mai navadhānatā bhavaṃ,

khamantu dausaṃ guṇataṃ nayantu vā||

yau sīhasūrau sitakuñ‍jarindau,

rājādhirājā ahu tambadīpai|

dubbāranāgādijitau narindau,

sukantabhīmādiguṇaupapan‍nau||

tan‍nāmadhaiyyau tadanubbādajātau,

saddhādiyuttau catusaitibhindau|

nāgādithāmau atiduppasayhau,

uḷārapañ‍ñau dhitimā yasassī||

taināhamac‍cantamanuggahītau,

anañ‍ñasādhāraṇasaṅgahaina|

saṅkhaipataukāsimimaṃ visuddhi-

saṃvaṇṇanaṃ sautuhitaṃ subaudhaṃ||

rājā pajaṃ rakkhatu sappajaṃva,

dhammañ‍ca laukāpi samācarantu|

pūraintu atthā supakappitā ca,

kālaina daivaupi pavassatūti||

abhidhānappadīpikāṭīkā niṭṭhitā|
